West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Open the window and enter the Kitchen early; +10 was recorded on that move. Bank easy surface items before descending.
2. Take the lantern, confirm it in inventory, turn it on, then go down. The descent recorded +25 here.
3. Carry the sword underground. This run had no weapon at the troll room and only took an axe nick, with no progress.
4. Use unexplored exits when a room is exhausted: Cellar south led to East Chasm and the Gallery, where 'take art' recorded +4.
5. After taking treasure, check inventory and score, then seek a verified route to the trophy case. Home return was never confirmed this run.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Never give treasure or gear to the troll or thief. 'Give troll painting' recorded -10 and a knockout that ended the episode.
2. Don't drop carried treasure to 'explore'. The painting was put down in the Troll room and Gallery with no score benefit.
3. Don't oscillate Cellar↔East Chasm, Gallery↔Studio or Cellar↔Troll room. After two repeats with no score, try a different action or untested exit.
4. Don't repeatedly re-enter the troll room unarmed or hope it stops blocking. Each entry risks axe damage and gains nothing.
5. Don't waste early steps on mailbox put/take, or on grabbing food and a manual without purpose. Confirm each item's use before spending a move.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Reach the house early and circle its exterior for entrances; a prior run opened the window into the Kitchen for +10 (not reproduced this run).
2. Take the lantern, confirm it in inventory, light it, then go down; a prior run recorded +25 on that descent.
3. Carry the sword underground. A prior unarmed troll-room visit gave only damage and no progress.
4. When a area is exhausted, test an unvisited exit. South House was the only new room this run, reached by Forest northwest.
5. After taking treasure, check inventory and score, then find and verify a route back to the trophy case; none was confirmed so far.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Never give treasure or gear to the troll or thief; 'give troll painting' recorded -10 and a knockout in a prior run.
2. Don't drop and re-take items like leaves or the painting as 'investigation'; this repeated many times with zero score effect.
3. Don't oscillate Clearing↔Forest Path↔Forest, Cellar↔East Chasm, Gallery↔Studio or Cellar↔Troll Room. After two repeats without score, pick an untried exit.
4. Don't wander the forest for hundreds of steps. After about 30 zero-score moves, go to the house and examine its exterior.
5. Don't use vague pseudo-directions like 'go around forest/trees' or re-enter the troll room unarmed. Use real compass exits and verify the new room name.
